# Data quality issue log

Each issue names a delivery and a record number. Record numbers are the 1-based ordinal of the record within its delivery under the record definition of the specification catalogue (section 2.2): headers, comments and control blocks are not counted, every record is. Disposition QUARANTINE removes that record from every use; ACCEPTED and FIXED-IN-SOURCE need no action. Delivery names are matched without regard to letter case; issues naming deliveries of other cycles do not apply.
